# Hebrew review for grammar and for ElevenLabs

Made by `tools/hebrew_voice_review.py` from `hebrew-review.csv`. **Nothing here is in the pack yet.** Rows with a grammar problem come first (586); then every spoken text with its ElevenLabs version (2386 rows in all; texts that several activities share are one row). A row marked REVIEW in the explanation is a possible mistake that needs a person to decide. The whole list, one row for each text in the app, is `hebrew-master.csv`; the editor's rules are in `docs/agents/hebrew-notes.md`.
